# KFAB Database Workbooks - Structural Schema & Data Dictionary

This document provides a comprehensive structural reference for all **39 Microsoft Excel Workbooks** located in the `Database/` directory of the KFAB ERP ecosystem. 

Each workbook is cataloged by its category, physical path, worksheets, column definitions, data types, and supported data records.

---

## Executive Summary of Database Directory

| Category Folder | File Count | Main Business Purpose | Key Worksheets |
| :--- | :---: | :--- | :--- |
| **`Attendence/`** | 3 | Daily and monthly muster rolls for plant workers across fiscal years (2025, 2026, 2027) | Monthly Muster (Jan-Dec), Daily Logs |
| **`Employee Details/`** | 1 | Master personnel registry, statutory KYC, bank accounts, designations, departments | Employee Master, Contact Info, Salary Grades |
| **`Expenses/`** | 5 | Operating expenditures (OpEx), site utilities, transport, and contractor costs by plant unit | Monthly Expense Ledgers, Category Breakdown |
| **`Inventory/`** | 5 | Unit-wise raw material, plates, beams, consumables, and machinery stock levels | Raw Material Ledger, Inward/Outward Balances |
| **`others/`** | 4 | External stakeholders, clients, suppliers/creditors, consumers, and auxiliary directories | Master Directories, Contact Persons, Tax/GSTIN |
| **`Outward Register/`** | 1 | Dispatch and outward consignment registry, delivery challans, truck numbers, weighment | Outward Consignments, Gate Passes |
| **`Salary and Advance/`** | 15 | Monthly payroll, advances, deductions, overtime, and net disbursements across 5 units & 3 years | Monthly Payroll Ledgers (2025, 2026, 2027) |
| **`Sales/`** | 5 | Revenue, billing, sales invoices, tonnage dispatched, and client receivables by plant unit | Sales Ledger, GST Invoices, Receivables |
| **Total** | **39 Files** | **Complete Heavy Fabrication Manufacturing Database** | **Multi-Tab Worksheets** |

---
